# Phase 1 UUID migration: API identifier exposure audit

Audited 2026-10-16 (Asia/Kolkata), repository `feroz-hub/sbom`, branch `feat/native-user-management`, HEAD `2f48ff835d9e692fc84b31aaa951aad2495888ac`.

**Audit only.** No API, application code, database, dependency or Git branch/ref change was made. Sources were parsed without importing application modules or executing startup, dependencies, validators or requests. Only this document was added to the repository; existing work and audits 01–04 were preserved.

## Scope and evidence

- **432 application HTTP registrations:** 428 mounted in SBOM and four in the separate KEV service, including two repeated method/path registrations. There are 430 distinct service/method/path combinations. Framework-generated documentation routes are outside this resource-identifier inventory.
- **252 Pydantic class declarations**, including inline router models, transitive inheritance and imported/re-exported models. There are **480 integer-containing class/field entries**, including inherited repetitions: 157 resource-ID entries and 323 numeric non-ID entries. These are source counts, not counts of unique database IDs or wire fields.
- The graph expands main.py prefixes, explicit add_api_route aliases, both dynamic platform clone families, validation-session aliases and hidden/deprecated decorators. No source router with application HTTP routes was left unmatched to a mount; KEV remains a separate service.
- Every exposure row records method, resolved route, field, direction, current type, resource and exact file:line. `Rnnn` identifies a registration in the route catalog. `[]` denotes list elements. Optionality and unions are retained. Registration/field rows can repeat a field across aliases, nested responses, branches and directions.
- **V-SIGNATURE** means a declared integer HTTP parameter, including dependency-introduced query parameters. **V-SCHEMA** means a recursively bound Pydantic request/response field. **V-CODE-TYPE** means a returned serializer field whose type follows an explicit annotation or mapped column. **V-REVIEW** means a concrete producer/query/validator/export assignment was inspected against the persistent-model inventory. These are source checks, not live endpoint tests.
- **ENCODED-REF** means an ID transported as a string/header/export representation. **DOMAIN-REF** means external/document identity, not a local surrogate. **S/open shape** marks dict/Any containers whose arbitrary nested keys cannot be established as a fixed integer-ID contract.

The audit inspected tracked Python sources, followed mount/import aliases, recursively walked Pydantic fields and their bases, traced returned serializers/service methods, and reviewed raw projections, late body validation, reports and streams. It did not retrieve runtime OpenAPI or issue requests. Open JSON/provider/historical payloads remain explicit limits; absence of a fixed field declaration is not proof that a container never holds another ID.

## Findings affecting migration scope

1. Integer IDs cross the identity/tenant, project/product, logical SBOM/version, component, analysis, VEX, remediation, schedule/report, AI configuration and recommendation boundaries. Both list/detail and nested responses expose them. The field matrices enumerate the registration-specific uses.
2. **Version selectors are record IDs:** `version_a`, `version_b` and `version_id` select sbom_source rows. `row_version`, `expected_version`, `role_assignment_version`, `schema_version` and lifecycle_revision are version/concurrency counters, not UUID candidates. `sbom_type` is an FK to a lookup record; project_status is a numeric status value.
3. **Keyset cursor is an ID:** /api/runs and /api/sboms use integer cursor selectors and emit decimal X-Next-Cursor headers. page/page_size/offset/limit and total-count headers remain quantities. Sources: [app/routers/runs.py:82](../../../app/routers/runs.py#L82), [app/routers/runs.py:165](../../../app/routers/runs.py#L165), [app/routers/sboms_crud.py:1003](../../../app/routers/sboms_crud.py#L1003).
4. **Legacy spellings/encodings persist:** projectid/project_id, runId/run_id, generic `{id}`, scan_id meaning analysis_run.id, and string-typed run filters parsed with int(). Numeric search text also selects run IDs. /dashboard/trend application_ids means **projects.id** ([app/routers/dashboard.py:44](../../../app/routers/dashboard.py#L44)); the newer application picker returns **products.id** ([app/routers/dashboard_main.py:104](../../../app/routers/dashboard_main.py#L104)).
5. Workspace/session/job/batch identifiers already use UUID strings. Some older response schemas still allow int | str for workspace_id/validation_session_id ([app/schemas.py:211](../../../app/schemas.py#L211), line 212). CVE/GHSA, bom-ref/SPDX, PURL/CPE, provider model IDs, role/permission codes and external subjects remain domain identities, even when they contain digits.
6. VEX assigned_to uses `membership:<tenant_users.id>`, not plain iam_users.id. Encoder/parser: [app/services/vex/authorization.py:24](../../../app/services/vex/authorization.py#L24), line 31. User/created-by filters and X-User-Id can be attribution text. X-Tenant-ID is a string header selecting local tenant context, listed separately from integer JSON/query fields.
7. PDF body runId:int spans existing RunCache and AnalysisRun PK spaces. Eligibility checks AnalysisRun then RunCache; rendering tries RunCache then reconstructs AnalysisRun ([app/routers/pdf.py:26](../../../app/routers/pdf.py#L26), lines 130, 137). This audit preserves that distinction rather than equating the tables.
8. **VulnerabilityRemediationOut is declared twice** ([app/schemas.py:473](../../../app/schemas.py#L473), [app/schemas.py:751](../../../app/schemas.py#L751)). The earlier optional-id model is captured by AnalysisFindingOut.remediation before the later class replaces the module binding. Later direct imports use the required-id/project_id model. The audit preserves declaration-time nested binding.
9. Platform AI/lifecycle/advisor aliases and both validation-session alias families remain registered. Hidden legacy analysis and AI endpoints are included. Documentation visibility does not establish route absence.
10. GET /api/v1/ai/providers/available and GET /api/v1/ai/providers/available/{name} each have two registrations, from AI usage and AI credentials. Both appear in the coverage ledger. Runtime dispatch/OpenAPI collision behavior was not exercised or changed.

## Structured error envelopes

Resource IDs also occur on failures. These are distinct from HTTP status/error-code numbers. The compare helper additions are in the raw matrix; arbitrary shared validation context is not treated as a fixed record-ID schema.

## Encoded IDs, SSE, reports and exports

SSE completion/progress IDs and lifecycle CSV/ZIP component IDs appear in the raw matrix. The transport envelope is text or file content rather than an ordinary JSON response model. The following string/header/search/export representations supplement those rows.

The POST SBOM analysis stream emits complete.data.runId from AnalysisRun.id ([app/routers/sboms_crud.py:2054](../../../app/routers/sboms_crud.py#L2054)); its total/severity/error/duration numbers are quantities. AI batch and legacy progress streams serialize BatchProgress.run_id ([app/ai/progress.py:66](../../../app/ai/progress.py#L66); serialization [app/routers/ai_fixes.py:882](../../../app/routers/ai_fixes.py#L882)). batch_id is a UUID string. No stream was opened during the audit.

Report/export coverage also includes the following format-specific distinctions:

| Method | Surface | Resource-ID fields or representation | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| POST | `/api/projects/{project_id}/reports/fda-510k-sbom/export` | Integer project path and nested selections[].sbom_id, findings_analysis_run_id, lifecycle_analysis_run_id. Internal report joins are not automatically asserted as exposed workbook ID cells. | [app/routers/reports.py:30](../../../app/routers/reports.py#L30), lines 31–33, 77, 83 |
| POST | `/api/pdf-report` | Body runId:int selects existing cache/run spaces; PDF is binary. Report CVE identifiers are natural identities, not finding PKs. | [app/routers/pdf.py:26](../../../app/routers/pdf.py#L26), lines 130, 137 |
| POST | `/api/v1/compare/{cache_key}/export` | cache_key is a hash string. JSON contains CompareResult's run/SBOM/project/product summary IDs; filenames and Markdown headings embed run IDs. | [app/services/compare_export.py:28](../../../app/services/compare_export.py#L28), lines 34, 40, 52 |
| GET | `/api/analysis-runs/{run_id}/export/sarif`, `/export/csv` | Integer run selector and filename. SARIF ruleId/rules[].id and CSV vuln_id are external vulnerability identities, not analysis_finding.id. | [app/routers/analysis.py:188](../../../app/routers/analysis.py#L188), lines 222, 249, 307, 313, 425 |
| GET | `/api/sboms/{id}/lifecycle/report`, `/reports/lifecycle-pack` | JSON sbom_id and components[].id; lifecycle CSV component_id is decimal text from sbom_component.id; ZIP retains those members. | [app/services/lifecycle/lifecycle_enrichment_service.py:421](../../../app/services/lifecycle/lifecycle_enrichment_service.py#L421), lines 705, 801; [app/routers/sbom_versions.py:405](../../../app/routers/sbom_versions.py#L405) |
| GET | `/api/sboms/{sbom_id}/reports/vulnerabilities.xlsx` | Integer SBOM selector. Workbook vulnerability id is provider/CVE/advisory identity; the column name alone does not expose a database finding surrogate. | [app/routers/sbom_versions.py:585](../../../app/routers/sbom_versions.py#L585); [app/services/sbom_vulnerability_excel_report_service.py:137](../../../app/services/sbom_vulnerability_excel_report_service.py#L137), line 245 |
| GET | `/api/sboms/{sbom_id}/vex/report`, `/reports/vex-pack` | Integer SBOM selector; VEX serializers can expose statement/component row IDs alongside external vulnerability/provenance identities. Formats are separate from the request selector. | [app/services/lifecycle/vex_provider.py:1168](../../../app/services/lifecycle/vex_provider.py#L1168); handlers in route catalog |
| GET | `/api/report-deliveries`, `/api/report-deliveries/{delivery_id}/artifacts/{artifact_id}` | Integer delivery/subscription/artifact response IDs and path selectors. Authenticated artifact paths identify records independently of the retained file's content/filename. | [app/routers/report_notifications.py:314](../../../app/routers/report_notifications.py#L314), lines 315, 330, 345 |
| GET | SBOM/workspace/job downloads and `/api/sboms/{id}/export` | SBOM/version selectors are integer; workspace/job selectors are UUID strings. Source bom-ref/SPDX/serial-number references retain document semantics. | [app/routers/sbom_validation_sessions.py:111](../../../app/routers/sbom_validation_sessions.py#L111); download/export registrations below |

Native authentication's JWT subject is a string encoding of a local user ID; numeric-subject validation is at [app/services/native_jwt_service.py:129](../../../app/services/native_jwt_service.py#L129), lines 130–133. This is a token compatibility boundary, not an integer JSON schema field. No tokens were generated or inspected.

## String-typed route boundaries

These 266 signature/dependency rows are not all integer IDs. UUID/session/batch/job, CVE/advisory, dedupe/package and actor strings remain separate kinds. Run-list/aggregate sbom_id/project_id/product_id are string-typed and parsed by _coerce_optional_int (app/routers/runs.py:45 and 89). X-Tenant-ID rows reflect explicit dependency signatures, not a guarantee that every other context path avoids reading headers manually.

## Raw request bodies and contextual identifier names

Report-subscription PATCH accepts a dict, then validates it through ReportPreferences.model_validate ([app/routers/report_notifications.py:192](../../../app/routers/report_notifications.py#L192)); its project_id/product_id/sbom_id integer fields are explicitly included in the raw matrix as IN body. The raw VEX import body is a document dictionary ([app/routers/vex.py:44](../../../app/routers/vex.py#L44)); document bom-ref/advisory/product references are not assumed to be database row IDs. Manual VEX override dictionaries carry status/evidence data while component/SBOM identifiers come from the route ([app/routers/vex.py:161](../../../app/routers/vex.py#L161); normalization [app/services/lifecycle/vex_provider.py:696](../../../app/services/lifecycle/vex_provider.py#L696)).

The name source_document_id has two proven meanings: import output carries external document provenance ([app/services/lifecycle/vex_provider.py:479](../../../app/services/lifecycle/vex_provider.py#L479)), whereas previous_decisions[].source_document_id is assigned statement.vex_document_id, a local integer reference ([app/services/vex/version_evidence.py:117](../../../app/services/vex/version_evidence.py#L117)). Both interpretations are retained; only the latter is included as an integer row reference. CVE scan_id resolves AnalysisRun directly ([app/services/cve_service.py:197](../../../app/services/cve_service.py#L197)).

Authorization request role_codes and permission_codes are semantic strings. Their corresponding role/permission record IDs in paths and responses are listed separately. X-Total-Count and similar header values are counts even though their transport representation is decimal text. Schema-only declarations, open JSON shapes, binary artifacts and namespaced/string identities are not silently recast as integer PK fields.

## Verification and unchanged scope

Every discovered HTTP decorator and explicit alias, plus both dynamic platform clone families, was accounted for in the mount graph. Numeric schema coverage follows transitive inheritance and declaration-time binding of the duplicated remediation class. Raw projections, explicit validators, report/export boundaries and SSE/header additions were reviewed against the primary-key/relationship inventory. File:line links, route/field row coverage and Markdown structure were checked.

This is a static exposure audit, not runtime authorization testing, HTTP execution, generated-client compatibility verification or proof that arbitrary provider/historical JSON has no additional ID keys. No application startup, database connection, API/code/schema change, dependency installation, checkout, pull, reset, stash, commit or push occurred. Existing tracked changes, the untracked frontend test and audits 01–04 were preserved. Only docs/uuid-migration/phase1/05-api-exposure.md was added to the repository.
